set_variable = { APDC_eco_eq_progress_153 = 0 }
set_country_flag = APDC_eco_eq_153

}

}

APDC_eco_produce_equipment = {
set_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_1 = modifier@production_factory_max_efficiency_factor }
add_to_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_1 = 0.5 }
clamp_temp_variable = { var = APDC_eco_math_1 min = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_eco_private_efficiency = APDC_eco_math_1 }
set_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_2 = APDC_eco_private_mil }
multiply_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_2 = 3.5 }
set_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_3 = modifier@industrial_capacity_factory }
add_to_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_3 = 1 }
clamp_temp_variable = { var = APDC_eco_math_3 min = 0 }
multiply_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_2 = APDC_eco_math_3 }
multiply_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_2 = APDC_eco_private_efficiency }
set_variable = { APDC_eco_private_ic = APDC_eco_math_2 }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_eco_equipment_active^num > 0 } }
set_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_4 = APDC_eco_private_ic }
divide_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_4 = APDC_eco_equipment_active^num }
set_variable = { APDC_eco_ic_per_type = APDC_eco_math_4 }

}

clamp_variable = { var = APDC_eco_purchase_quantity min = 0 max = APDC_eco_purchase_stock }
set_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_325 = APDC_eco_purchase_quantity }
divide_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_325 = 1000 }
set_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m = APDC_eco_math_325 }
set_variable = { APDC_eco_round_original = APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m }
round_variable = APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m > APDC_eco_round_original } } subtract_from_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m = 1 } }
set_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_326 = APDC_eco_purchase_quantity }
modulo_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_326 = 1000 }
multiply_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_326 = APDC_eco_purchase_unit_k }
set_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_tail = APDC_eco_math_326 }
set_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_327 = APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m }
multiply_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_327 = APDC_eco_purchase_unit_k }
set_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m = APDC_eco_math_327 }
set_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_328 = APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m }
modulo_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_328 = 1 }
multiply_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_328 = 1000 }
add_to_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_328 = APDC_eco_purchase_tail }
set_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_cost_k = APDC_eco_math_328 }
set_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_329 = APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m }
set_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m = APDC_eco_math_329 }
set_variable = { APDC_eco_round_original = APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m }
round_variable = APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m > APDC_eco_round_original } } subtract_from_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m = 1 } }
set_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_330 = APDC_eco_purchase_cost_k }
divide_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_330 = 1000 }
set_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_carry = APDC_eco_math_330 }
set_variable = { APDC_eco_round_original = APDC_eco_purchase_carry }
round_variable = APDC_eco_purchase_carry
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_carry > APDC_eco_round_original } } subtract_from_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_carry = 1 } }
add_to_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m = APDC_eco_purchase_carry }
set_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_331 = APDC_eco_purchase_carry }
multiply_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_331 = -1000 }
add_to_temp_variable = { APDC_eco_math_331 = APDC_eco_purchase_cost_k }
set_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_cost_k = APDC_eco_math_331 }

}
APDC_eco_purchase_equipment = {
APDC_eco_prepare_purchase = yes
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_quantity > 0 } NOT = { check_variable = { APDC_eco_cash_m < 0 } } OR = { check_variable = { APDC_eco_cash_m > APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m } AND = { check_variable = { APDC_eco_cash_m = APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m } NOT = { check_variable = { APDC_eco_cash_k < APDC_eco_purchase_cost_k } } } } }
subtract_from_variable = { APDC_eco_cash_m = APDC_eco_purchase_cost_m }
subtract_from_variable = { APDC_eco_cash_k = APDC_eco_purchase_cost_k }
APDC_eco_normalize_cash = yes
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_eco_purchase_id = 1 } }
